They now keep their real values.\n\n**Wrong colour**\n- **Tees behind a wall:** a tee just behind a wall turned the line yellow (the \"a tee\" colour), even though the hook stops at the wall. Tees are now only checked up to where the hook hits the map.\n- **Tees counted only when very close:** the line counted a tee as hit only within half the distance the real hook uses, so it could miss tees the hook would grab.
